No teaching — a fresh-eyes verdict gate that maps findings to SOC 2 Common Criteria (CC1–CC9), OWASP Top 10, PCI DSS v4 (when payment data is present), NIST 800-63B (auth), multi-tenant isolation, PII/encryption hygiene, and dependency CVEs. Auto-detects stack + conventions from CLAUDE.md + language signals. Posts inline GitHub PR review comments + a verdict (SHIP / SHIP WITH FIXES / DO NOT SHIP / DO NOT SHIP — COMPLIANCE BLOCKER).
